Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cfl operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 14. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 498 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 14 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cfl structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 498. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
